m.register_forward_hook(self._hook_fn)\n        log.info(\"Hooked layer 6 (type=%s, id=%d)\", type(m).__name__, id(m))\n\n    def _hook_fn(self, _module, _input, output):\n        if isinstance(output, tuple):\n            h = output[0]\n        else:\n            h = output\n        self._activation = h[:, 0].detach().float().cpu()\n\n    def get(self):\n        act = self._activation\n        self._activation = None\n        return act\n\n    def remove(self):\n        if self._hook:\n            self._hook.remove()\n            self._hook = None\n\n\ndef collect_from_perturbation(policy, collector, task_suite, perturbation_type,\n                              num_envs, steps_per_env, seed):\n    \"\"\"Collect activations from one perturbation type using real environments.\"\"\"\n    from evaluate_libero_plus import (\n        get_tasks_by_perturbation, obs_to_model_input,\n        NUM_STEPS_WAIT, LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION, IMAGE_SIZE,\n        PERTURBATION_TO_JSON_CATEGORY, _load_task_classification,\n        _extract_base_task_name,\n    )\n    from libero.libero import get_libero_path\n    from libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n\n    task_ids = get_tasks_by_perturbation(task_suite, perturbation_type)\n    if not task_ids:\n        log.warning(\"No tasks for %s/%s\", task_suite, perturbation_type)\n        return [], []\n\n    rng = random.Random(seed)\n    bddl_root = Path(get_libero_path(\"bddl_files\"))\n    init_root = Path(get_libero_path(\"init_states\"))\n\n    activations = []\n    metadata = []\n    env_count = 0\n    fail_count = 0\n\n    shuffled_tasks = list(task_ids)\n    rng.shuffle(shuffled_tasks)\n\n    for task_id in shuffled_tasks:\n        if env_count >= num_envs:\n            break\n\n        if not isinstance(task_id, str):\n            continue\n\n        task_name = task_id\n        task_bddl_file = bddl_root / task_suite / f\"{task_name}.bddl\"\n        if not task_bddl_file.exists():\n            continue\n\n        base_name = _extract_base_task_name(task_name)\n        task_description = base_name.replace(\"_\", \" \")\n\n        try:\n            env = OffScreenRenderEnv(\n                bddl_file_name=str(task_bddl_file),\n                camera_heights=IMAGE_SIZE,\n                camera_widths=IMAGE_SIZE,\n            )\n            env.seed(seed + env_count)\n        except Exception as e:\n            log.debug(\"Failed to create env for %s: %s\", task_name, e)\n            fail_count += 1\n            continue\n\n        try:\n            # Try loading init states from base task\n            init_path = init_root / task_suite / f\"{base_name}.pruned_init\"\n            use_reset = False\n            if init_path.exists():\n                init_states = torch.load(str(init_path), weights_only=False)\n                trial_idx = rng.randint(0, len(init_states) - 1)\n                try:\n                    obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[trial_idx])\n                except (ValueError, RuntimeError):\n                    use_reset = True\n            else:\n                use_reset = True\n\n            if use_reset:\n                obs = env.reset()\n\n            # Wait steps for physics to settle\n            for _ in range(NUM_STEPS_WAIT):\n                obs, _, _, _ = env.step(LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION)\n\n            # Collect activations at multiple timesteps\n            for step in range(steps_per_env):\n                obs_dict = obs_to_model_input(obs, task_description)\n                _ = policy.infer(obs_dict)\n                act = collector.get()\n\n                if act is not None:\n                    activations.append(act.squeeze(0))\n                    metadata.append({\n                        \"perturbation_type\": perturbation_type,\n                        \"class_3\": PERTURBATION_TO_3CLASS[perturbation_type],\n                        \"task_suite\": task_suite,\n                        \"task_name\": task_name,\n                        \"step\": step,\n                    })\n\n                obs, _, done, _ = env.step(LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION)\n                if done:\n                    break\n\n            env_count += 1\n        except Exception as e:\n            log.debug(\"Error collecting from %s: %s\", task_name, e)\n            fail_count += 1\n        finally:\n            env.close()\n\n        if env_count % 10 == 0 and env_count > 0:\n            log.info(\"  [%s] %d/%d envs, %d activations, %d failures\",\n                    perturbation_type, env_count, num_envs, len(activations), fail_count)\n\n    log.info(\"  [%s] Final: %d envs, %d activations, %d failures\",\n            perturbation_type, env_count, len(activations), fail_count)\n    return activations, metadata\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--device\", default=\"cuda:0\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/activations_real_env\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--task_suite\", default=\"libero_spatial\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num_envs_per_perturbation\", type=int, default=40)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--steps_per_env\", type=int, default=8)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--checkpoint\", default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/pi0_libero_openpi\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    device = args.device\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    log.info(\"Loading policy on %s...\", device)\n    from openpi_metabrain_policy import OpenPIMetaBrainPolicy\n    policy_wrapper = OpenPIMetaBrainPolicy.from_checkpoint(\n        checkpoint_path=args.checkpoint,\n        routing_mode='none',\n        device=device,\n        use_delta_actions=True,\n    )\n    policy_wrapper._diag_max = 0\n\n    collector = Layer6Collector(policy_wrapper.model)\n    collector.register()\n\n    # Quick sanity check\n    from evaluate_libero_plus import obs_to_model_input\n    dummy_obs = {\n        \"agentview_image\": np.zeros((224, 224, 3), dtype=np.uint8),\n        \"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\": np.zeros((224, 224, 3), dtype=np.uint8),\n        \"robot0_eef_pos\": np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32),\n        \"robot0_eef_quat\": np.array([0, 0, 0, 1], dtype=np.float32),\n        \"robot0_gripper_qpos\": np.zeros(2, dtype=np.float32),\n    }\n    _ = policy_wrapper.infer(obs_to_model_input(dummy_obs, \"test\"))\n    test_act = collector.get()\n    if test_act is None:\n        log.error(\"Hook returned None!\")\n        return\n    log.info(\"Hook OK: dim=%d, norm=%.1f\", test_act.shape[-1], test_act.norm().item())\n\n    # Collect from each perturbation type\n    perturbation_types = [\n        \"background_textures\", \"camera_viewpoints\", \"light_conditions\", \"sensor_noise\",\n        \"objects_layout\", \"robot_initial_states\",\n    ]\n\n    # Balance: visual has 4 sub-types, so use fewer envs per sub-type\n    # Target ~1000 samples per 3-class category\n    visual_envs = args.num_envs_per_perturbation  # 40 envs × 8 steps = 320 per visual sub-type, 4×320=1280 total visual\n    other_envs = args.num_envs_per_perturbation * 3  # 120 envs × 8 steps = 960 per class\n    \n    samples_config = {\n        \"background_textures\": visual_envs,\n        \"camera_viewpoints\": visual_envs,\n        \"light_conditions\": visual_envs,\n        \"sensor_noise\": visual_envs,\n        \"objects_layout\": other_envs,\n        \"robot_initial_states\": other_envs,\n    }\n\n    all_activations = []\n    all_metadata = []\n\n    for ptype in perturbation_types:\n        n_envs = samples_config[ptype]\n        log.info(\"Collecting from %s (%d envs × %d steps)...\", ptype, n_envs, args.steps_per_env)\n        t0 = time.time()\n\n        acts, meta = collect_from_perturbation(\n            policy_wrapper, collector, args.task_suite, ptype,\n            n_envs, args.steps_per_env, args.seed\n        )\n\n        elapsed = time.time() - t0\n        log.info(\"  %s: %d activations in %.1fs (%.2f samples/s)\", \n                ptype, len(acts), elapsed, len(acts)/max(elapsed,1))\n        all_activations.extend(acts)\n        all_metadata.extend(meta)\n\n        # Save intermediate checkpoint\n        if all_activations:\n            X_tmp = torch.stack(all_activations)\n            labels_tmp = torch.tensor([CLASS_TO_IDX[m[\"class_3\"]] for m in all_metadata])\n            torch.save({\n                \"activations\": X_tmp,\n                \"labels_3class\": labels_tmp,\n                \"metadata\": all_metadata,\n            }, Path(args.output_dir) / \"activations_partial.pt\")\n\n    # Save final results\n    if not all_activations:\n        log.error(\"No activations collected!\")\n        return\n\n    X = torch.stack(all_activations)\n    labels_3class = torch.tensor([CLASS_TO_IDX[m[\"class_3\"]] for m in all_metadata])\n\n    class_counts = collections.Counter(labels_3class.numpy().tolist())\n    log.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    log.info(\"COLLECTION COMPLETE\")\n    log.info(\"Total samples: %d\", len(X))\n    log.info(\"Class distribution: visual=%d, spatial=%d, physical=%d\",\n             class_counts.get(0, 0), class_counts.get(1, 0), class_counts.get(2, 0))\n    log.info(\"Activation shape: %s\", X.shape)\n    log.info(\"Mean norm: %.1f, std norm: %.1f\", \n             X.norm(dim=-1).mean().item(), X.norm(dim=-1).std().item())\n    log.info(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    save_path = Path(args.output_dir) / \"activations.pt\"\n    torch.save({\n        \"activations\": X,\n        \"labels_3class\": labels_3class,\n        \"metadata\": all_metadata,\n        \"config\": {\n            \"task_suite\": args.task_suite,\n            \"num_envs_per_perturbation\": args.num_envs_per_perturbation,\n            \"steps_per_env\": args.steps_per_env,\n            \"seed\": args.seed,\n            \"device\": device,\n            \"hook_path\": \"paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.model.language_model.layers.6\",\n            \"pooling\": \"first_token\",\n            \"perturbation_types\": perturbation_types,\n        }\n    }, save_path)\n    log.info(\"Saved to %s\", save_path)\n\n    policy_wrapper.cleanup()\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nSCRIPT\necho \"Updated collection script {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_016RofT15eZuSyPwc1ugtoJC","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Now I understand the LIBERO-Plus perturbation mechanism:\n\nThe task name format is:\n`{base_task}_view_{horizon}_{vertical}_{scale}_{rot}_{vert}_initstate_{state_idx}[_noise_{noise}]`\n\nThe `OffScreenRenderEnv` constructor parses this:\n- Strips `_view_X_Y_Z_A_B` part to get the base BDDL file\n- Extracts camera view params (horizon_view, vertical_view, scale_factor, end_point_rot, end_point_vertical)\n\n\n- Extracts the init_state index and modifies the robot name accordingly if it's non-zero\n\nSo the perturbations are all encoded directly in the task name itself—camera viewpoints, robot initial states, and sensor noise are all specified through different parameters in the naming scheme. I need to pass the full perturbed task name to `OffScreenRenderEnv`, which will parse it to find the base BDDL file and apply the camera and initialization perturbations.\n\nLooking at how the constructor actually works, it expects the full path with all the perturbation suffixes, then splits on `_view_` to extract the base BDDL filename and parse out the perturbation parameters. I need to update my collection script to pass the complete task name rather than searching for separate BDDL files. The base BDDL files exist since they're the nominal tasks, so the solution is to pass the full perturbed task name with the `.bddl` extension to `OffScreenRenderEnv`, and let it handle the parsing by splitting on `_view_` internally. For sensor_noise tasks, I need to figure out how they're handled since they use a `_noise_` suffix instead.\n\nLooking at the task names from the classification file, I'm seeing that all perturbation types—camera viewpoints, robot initial states, and sensor noise—actually follow the same naming pattern with `_view_` and `_initstate_` parameters, where the perturbation values are encoded in those parameters themselves. So my script needs to check if the base task exists and then construct the full perturbed filename accordingly, rather than trying to handle each perturbation type > /root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase0/collect_activations_real_env.py << 'SCRIPT'\n\"\"\"\nCollect PaliGemma layer-6 activations from REAL LIBERO-Plus environments.\n\nLIBERO-Plus encodes perturbations in task names:\n  {base}_view_{h}_{v}_{scale}_{rot}_{vert}_initstate_{idx}[_noise_{n}]\nOffScreenRenderEnv parses this internally to apply camera/robot/noise perturbations.\n\nOutput: /root/autodl-tmp/activations_real_env/activations.pt\n\"\"\"\n\nimport os\nos.environ[\"JAXTYPING_DISABLE\"] = \"1\"\nos.environ[\"MUJOCO_GL\"] = \"egl\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport collections\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport random\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\n\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase1\")\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/root/meta_brain_lora_routing\")\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/root/openpi/src\")\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/root/openpi\")\n\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\")\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nPERTURBATION_TO_3CLASS = {\n    \"camera_viewpoints\": \"visual\",\n    \"light_conditions\": \"visual\",\n    \"background_textures\": \"visual\",\n    \"sensor_noise\": \"visual\",\n    \"objects_layout\": \"spatial\",\n    \"robot_initial_states\": \"physical\",\n}\n\nCLASS_TO_IDX = {\"visual\": 0, \"spatial\": 1, \"physical\": 2}\n\n\nclass Layer6Collector:\n    def __init__(self, model):\n        self.model = model\n        self._hook = None\n        self._activation = None\n\n    def register(self):\n        module_path = \"paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.model.language_model.layers.6\"\n        m = self.model\n        for p in module_path.split(\".\"):\n            m = getattr(m, p)\n        self._hook = m.register_forward_hook(self._hook_fn)\n        log.info(\"Hooked layer 6 (type=%s)\", type(m).__name__)\n\n    def _hook_fn(self, _module, _input, output):\n        if isinstance(output, tuple):\n            h = output[0]\n        else:\n            h = output\n        self._activation = h[:, 0].detach().float().cpu()\n\n    def get(self):\n        act = self._activation\n        self._activation = None\n        return act\n\n    def remove(self):\n        if self._hook:\n            self._hook.remove()\n            self._hook = None\n\n\ndef collect_from_perturbation(policy, collector, task_suite, perturbation_type,\n                              num_envs, steps_per_env, seed):\n    \"\"\"Collect activations from one perturbation type.\"\"\"\n    from evaluate_libero_plus import (\n        get_tasks_by_perturbation, obs_to_model_input,\n        NUM_STEPS_WAIT, LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION, IMAGE_SIZE,\n        _extract_base_task_name,\n    )\n    from libero.libero import get_libero_path\n    from libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n\n    task_ids = get_tasks_by_perturbation(task_suite, perturbation_type)\n    if not task_ids:\n        log.warning(\"No tasks for %s/%s\", task_suite, perturbation_type)\n        return [], []\n\n    rng = random.Random(seed)\n    bddl_root = Path(get_libero_path(\"bddl_files\"))\n    init_root = Path(get_libero_path(\"init_states\"))\n\n    activations = []\n    metadata = []\n    env_count = 0\n    fail_count = 0\n\n    shuffled_tasks = list(task_ids)\n    rng.shuffle(shuffled_tasks)\n\n    for task_id in shuffled_tasks:\n        if env_count >= num_envs:\n            break\n\n        if not isinstance(task_id, str):\n            continue\n\n        task_name = task_id\n        base_name = _extract_base_task_name(task_name)\n        task_description = base_name.replace(\"_\", \" \")\n\n        # Determine BDDL path:\n        # Tasks with _view_..._initstate_... encode perturbation in name\n        # OffScreenRenderEnv parses them internally\n        # Tasks with explicit BDDL files (background_textures, objects_layout, light_conditions)\n        # have their own .bddl\n        has_view_encoding = \"_view_\" in task_name and \"_initstate_\" in task_name\n        \n        if has_view_encoding:\n            # Pass full name; OffScreenRenderEnv will split on _view_ to find base BDDL\n            bddl_path = str(bddl_root / task_suite / f\"{task_name}.bddl\")\n            # Check base BDDL exists\n            base_bddl = bddl_root / task_suite / f\"{base_name}.bddl\"\n            if not base_bddl.exists():\n                continue\n        else:\n            # Direct BDDL file (background textures, objects layout, light conditions)\n            bddl_path = str(bddl_root / task_suite / f\"{task_name}.bddl\")\n            if not Path(bddl_path).exists():\n                continue\n\n        try:\n            env = OffScreenRenderEnv(\n                bddl_file_name=bddl_path,\n                camera_heights=IMAGE_SIZE,\n                camera_widths=IMAGE_SIZE,\n            )\n            env.seed(seed + env_count)\n        except Exception as e:\n            log.debug(\"Failed to create env for %s: %s\", task_name[:60], e)\n            fail_count += 1\n            continue\n\n        try:\n            # For view-encoded tasks, use env.reset() (the perturbation is in env config)\n            # For tasks with separate BDDL, try init states\n            use_reset = has_view_encoding\n            \n            if not use_reset:\n                init_path = init_root / task_suite / f\"{base_name}.pruned_init\"\n                if init_path.exists():\n                    init_states = torch.load(str(init_path), weights_only=False)\n                    trial_idx = rng.randint(0, len(init_states) - 1)\n                    try:\n                        obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[trial_idx])\n                    except (ValueError, RuntimeError):\n                        use_reset = True\n                else:\n                    use_reset = True\n\n            if use_reset:\n                obs = env.reset()\n\n            # Wait steps\n            for _ in range(NUM_STEPS_WAIT):\n                obs, _, _, _ = env.step(LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION)\n\n            # Collect activations\n            for step in range(steps_per_env):\n                obs_dict = obs_to_model_input(obs, task_description)\n                _ = policy.infer(obs_dict)\n                act = collector.get()\n\n                if act is not None:\n                    activations.append(act.squeeze(0))\n                    metadata.append({\n                        \"perturbation_type\": perturbation_type,\n                        \"class_3\": PERTURBATION_TO_3CLASS[perturbation_type],\n                        \"task_suite\": task_suite,\n                        \"task_name\": task_name[:80],\n                        \"step\": step,\n                    })\n\n                obs, _, done, _ = env.step(LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION)\n                if done:\n                    break\n\n            env_count += 1\n        except Exception as e:\n            log.debug(\"Error: %s: %s\", task_name[:50], str(e)[:100])\n            fail_count += 1\n        finally:\n            env.close()\n\n        if env_count % 10 == 0 and env_count > 0:\n            log.info(\"  [%s] %d/%d envs, %d acts, %d fails\",\n                    perturbation_type, env_count, num_envs, len(activations), fail_count)\n\n    log.info(\"  [%s] Final: %d envs, %d activations, %d failures\",\n            perturbation_type, env_count, len(activations), fail_count)\n    return activations, metadata\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--device\", default=\"cuda:0\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/activations_real_env\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--task_suite\", default=\"libero_spatial\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num_envs_per_perturbation\", type=int, default=40)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--steps_per_env\", type=int, default=8)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--checkpoint\", default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/pi0_libero_openpi\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    device = args.device\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    log.info(\"Loading policy on %s...\", device)\n    from openpi_metabrain_policy import OpenPIMetaBrainPolicy\n    policy_wrapper = OpenPIMetaBrainPolicy.from_checkpoint(\n        checkpoint_path=args.checkpoint,\n        routing_mode='none',\n        device=device,\n        use_delta_actions=True,\n    )\n    policy_wrapper._diag_max = 0\n\n    collector = Layer6Collector(policy_wrapper.model)\n    collector.register()\n\n    # Quick sanity check\n    from evaluate_libero_plus import obs_to_model_input\n    dummy_obs = {\n        \"agentview_image\": np.zeros((224, 224, 3), dtype=np.uint8),\n        \"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\": np.zeros((224, 224, 3), dtype=np.uint8),\n        \"robot0_eef_pos\": np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32),\n        \"robot0_eef_quat\": np.array([0, 0, 0, 1], dtype=np.float32),\n        \"robot0_gripper_qpos\": np.zeros(2, dtype=np.float32),\n    }\n    _ = policy_wrapper.infer(obs_to_model_input(dummy_obs, \"test\"))\n    test_act = collector.get()\n    if test_act is None:\n        log.error(\"Hook returned None!\")\n        return\n    log.info(\"Hook OK: dim=%d, norm=%.1f\", test_act.shape[-1], test_act.norm().item())\n\n    # Collect from each perturbation type\n    # For balanced classes:\n    # - visual (4 sub-types): fewer envs each, total ~1000\n    # - spatial: ~1000\n    # - physical: ~1000\n    perturbation_types = [\n        \"background_textures\", \"camera_viewpoints\", \"light_conditions\", \"sensor_noise\",\n        \"objects_layout\", \"robot_initial_states\",\n    ]\n\n    visual_envs = args.num_envs_per_perturbation\n    other_envs = args.num_envs_per_perturbation * 3\n\n    samples_config = {\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01G8oNxvEjXiADZvUXk4CD51","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"L2 normalization helps slightly (F1 going up to 0.43-0.46 range with higher learning rates). Let me wait for the full result. But this is still very low compared to the target of 0.95.\n\nThis strongly suggests that the REAL LIBERO-Plus perturbations don't produce sufficiently different activations at layer 6 of this model. The model is robust to these perturbations in terms of its internal representations.\n\nThis is actually the SAME problem as with synthetic perturbations: PaliGemma is robust to both real and synthetic perturbations, so the activations don't change much.\n \nI should explore a few different approaches: trying earlier layers that might be more sensitive to visual changes, examining LayerNorm outputs instead of residual streams, or working with image embeddings before the language model processes them. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01LWHNSbGxDbkdjD9yaip7bP","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Now I understand! The Phase 0 configuration used:\n1. **Multiple PaliGemma layers**: [2, 4, 6, 8, 10, 12, 14, 16] - 8 layers!\n2. **Mean pooling** over the full sequence\n3. **Concatenation** of activations from all layers → input_dim = 8 × 2048 = 16384\n\nThis is VERY different from what I'm doing (single layer 6, first-token).\n\nAlso critical:\n\n\nThe layer path uses `language_model.layers.{i}` without `model.` in the middle, though both paths should point to the same module. The real insight is that Phase 0's approach of concatenating activations from 8 different layers with mean pooling over the sequence produced that highly discriminative 16384-dimensional feature vector (F1=0.9963), whereas my current approach only uses layer 6 with first-token extraction.\n\nThe deployed PAR Router actually hooks just layer 6 with first-token pooling (2048-dim), so the actual router performance might be lower than that initial feasibility test. I need to check what the existing `best_router.pt` checkpoint expects and whether the Phase 0 data was collected with a different script version.\n\nThe real issue is that Phase 0 used synthetic perturbations which were easy to classify, but the router doesn't generalize to real perturbations—my actual environment data with single layer 6 only achieves F1=0.39 because the model's internal representations don't strongly differentiate between real perturbation types with just first-token pooling. I could improve this by switching to mean pooling over the full sequence to capture more information, or by incorporating multiple layers for better discrimination.\n\nFor the PAR Router to actually work, I need to ensure it uses the same features at both training and inference—the eval script uses first-token from layer 6, so I should either retrain with mean pooling (which would require updating the inference code) or verify my approach using the v3 test data that was previously successful. Let me check what F1 score Phase 0 achieved with first-token pooling to understand the {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01TUZV13HpNiwaDkTaRJDdo5","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Very interesting! The Phase 0 \"final\" results with `first` pooling + `paligemma_only` got 99.1% accuracy. But this was on the v3 data (synthetic perturbations from parquet).\n\nThe key issue is clear:\n1. Synthetic perturbations → easily separable (F1=0.99) because the perturbations are strong and artificial\n2. Real LIBERO-Plus environments → not separable (F1=0.39) because the model is robust to these perturbations\n\nBut wait - the `paligemma_only` with `first` pooling uses MULTIPLE layers (2,4,6,8,10,12,14,16). \n\nSo the input dimension is 8 layers × 2048 = 16384. That's a lot more capacity than my single-layer approach with just 2048 dimensions. The real breakthrough here is that I need to extract features from multiple layers and concatenate them together.\n\nDifferent perturbation types likely have distinct signatures across the network's depth, so capturing activations from all 8 layers gives the classifier much richer information to work with. I'll modify my collection script to hook into all the PaliGemma layers and retrain the classifier on the full 16384-dimensional feature   [i for i in PALIGEMMA_LAYERS if i < n_layers])\n\n    def _make_hook(self, layer_idx):\n        def hook_fn(_module, _input, output):\n            h = output[0] if isinstance(output, tuple) else collect_from_perturbation(policy, collector, task_suite, perturbation_type,\n                              num_envs, steps_per_env, seed):\n    from evaluate_libero_plus import (\n from libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n\n    task_ids = get_tasks_by_perturbation(task_suite, perturbation_type)\n    if not task_ids:\n        return [], []\n\n    rng = fail_count = 0\n\n    shuffled_tasks = list(task_ids)\n    rng.shuffle(shuffled_tasks)\n\n    for task_id in shuffled_tasks:\n        if env_count >= num_envs:\n            break\n        if not   continue\n\n        try:\n            use_reset = has_view_encoding\n            if not use_reset:\n                init_path = init_root / task_suite / f\"{base_name}.pruned_init\"\n                if init_path.exists():\n                    init_states = torch.load(str(init_path), weights_only=False)\n                    trial_idx = rng.randint(0, len(init_states) - 1)\n                    try:\n                        obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[trial_idx])\n                    except (ValueError, RuntimeError):\n                        use_reset = True\n                else:\n                    use_reset = True\n\n            if use_reset:\n                obs = env.reset()\n\n            for _ in range(NUM_STEPS_WAIT):\n                obs, _, _, _ = env.step(LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION)\n\n            for step in range(steps_per_env):\n                obs_dict = obs_to_model_input(obs, task_description)\n                _ = policy.infer(obs_dict)\n                act = collector.get()\n\n                if act is not None:\n                    activations.append(act)\n                    metadata.append({\n                        \"perturbation_type\": perturbation_type,\n                        \"class_3\": PERTURBATION_TO_3CLASS[perturbation_type],\n                        \"task_suite\": task_suite,\n                        \"task_name\": task_name[:80],\n                        \"step\": step,\n                    })\n\n                obs, _, done, _ = env.step(LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION)\n                if done:\n                    break\n\n            env_count += 1\n        except Exception:\n            fail_count += 1\n        finally:\n            env.close()\n\n        if env_count % 10 == 0 and env_count > 0:\n            log.info(\"  [%s] %d/%d envs, %d acts, %d fails\",\n                    perturbation_type, env_count, num_envs, len(activations), fail_count)\n\n    log.info(\"  [%s] Final: %d envs, %d activations, %d failures\",\n            perturbation_type, env_count, len(activations), fail_count)\n    return activations, metadata\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--device\", default=\"cuda:0\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/activations_real_env_multilayer\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--task_suite\", default=\"libero_spatial\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num_envs_per_perturbation\", type=int, default=40)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--steps_per_env\", evaluate_libero_plus import obs_to_model_input\n    dummy_obs = {\n        \"agentview_image\": np.zeros((224, 224, 3), dtype=np.uint8),\n        \"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\": np.zeros((224, 224, 3), \"light_conditions\", \"sensor_noise\",\n        \"objects_layout\", \"robot_initial_states\",\n    ]\n    visual_envs = args.num_envs_per_perturbation\n    other_envs = args.num_envs_per_perturbation * 3\n    samples_config = {\n        \"background_textures\": visual_envs,\n        \"camera_viewpoints\": visual_envs,\n        \"light_conditions\": visual_envs,\n        \"sensor_noise\": visual_envs,\n        \"objects_layout\": other_envs,\n        \"robot_initial_states\": other_envs,\n    }\n\n    all_activations = []\n    all_metadata = []\n\n    for ptype in perturbation_types:\n Fix: use `/root/miniconda3/bin/python` (python 3.12)\n   - **SSH 30s timeout**: MCP ssh_execute tool has a hard 30s limit. Fix: use `nohup ... &` for background execution, check logs with separate commands\n   - **Layer index out of range (18, 24)**: Model only has 18 layers (0-17). Fix: only hook layers within range\n   - **Activation norm ~54-80k instead of expected ~250**: The residual stream norm is naturally high. The \"~250\" was likely from layernorm output or different data. The input_layernorm at layer 6 showed norm=86. This doesn't prevent classification IF using multi-layer features.\n   - **objects_layout init_states dimension mismatch**: `ValueError: could not broadcast input array from shape (36,) into shape (49,)`. Fix: catch ValueError/RuntimeError and fallback to `env.reset()`\n   - **camera_viewpoints/sensor_noise/robot_initial_states: 0 BDDL files**: These perturbations are encoded in task names, not separate files. Fix: detect `_view_` + `_initstate_` pattern and pass full name to OffScreenRenderEnv which parses it internally\n   - **Single layer-6 router F1=0.39**: Not enough discriminative power in 2048-dim single layer. Fix: use multi-layer (8 layers × 2048 = 16384-dim) as Phase 0 did\n\n5. Problem Solving:\n   - Successfully collected 3200 real-env activations across all 6 perturbation types\n   - Identified that single-layer approach is insufficient (F1=0.39-0.46)\n   - Found that Phase 0's high F1 used 8-layer concatenation (16384 dim)\n   - Created multi-layer collection script but haven't run it yet\n   - The deployed PARRouterLite only hooks one layer - this will need updating if multi-layer router works\n\n6. All user messages:\n   - The initial task message with full instructions for collecting activations from real LIBERO-Plus environments and training the PAR router\n   - \"CRITICAL: Respond with TEXT ONLY...\" (the current summarization request)\n\n7. Pending Tasks:\n   - Run `collect_activations_real_env_multilayer.py` to collect 16384-dim multi-layer activations\n   - Train router on multi-layer data (need to update `train_router_real_env.py` for input_dim=16384)\n   - If multi-layer F1 ≥ 0.95, update PARRouterLite in eval to hook all 8 layers\n   - If F1 still low, investigate further (different pooling, different feature extraction)\n   - Register final artifacts\n\n8. Current Work:\n   I had just written `/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase0/collect_activations_real_env_multilayer.py` which hooks 8 PaliGemma layers [2,4,6,8,10,12,14,16] and concatenates first-token activations to produce 16384-dim feature vectors. This was motivated by discovering that Phase 0's high F1 (0.991 with first-token, 0.993 with mean pooling) came from using all 8 layers concatenated, not just layer 6.\n\n   The single-layer (2048-dim) approach failed with F1=0.39 (unnormalized) / 0.42-0.46 (L2 normalized). The multi-layer script has been written but NOT yet executed.\n\n   The normalized training run (PID 492259) may still be running on the server.\n\n   Collected data location: `/root/autodl-tmp/activations_real_env/activations.pt` (3200 samples, [3200, 2048], single layer 6)\n\n9.
